    CHAIRMAN’S LETTER
    主席信札


    Dear Shareholders,                                                         各位尊敬的股東：

    2022 was a landmark year for the Group as we continued on our              二零二二年是本集團的里程碑。儘管全球經
    strong growth trajectory and achieved remarkable operating results,        濟及地緣政治面臨一系列挑戰，包括通脹高
    despite a host of global economic and geopolitical challenges,             企、主 要 央 行 實 行 貨 幣 緊 縮 政 策 及 加 息、中
    including high inflation, monetary tightening policies and interest rate   國新冠疫情反覆及迭加俄烏衝突，但我們仍
    rises by key central banks, the resurgence of the COVID pandemic in        能保持強勁的增長軌跡並取得了亮麗的經營
    China as well as the Russia-Ukraine conflict.                              業績。

    I am incredibly proud of our outstanding team to deliver better            對於我們優秀的團隊在二零二二年取得超
    than expected results in 2022. Revenue of key business segments            出 預 期 的 業 績，本 人 感 到 無 比 自 豪。主 要 業
    (including food additives, animal nutrition and colloid) increased         務分部（包括食品添加劑、動物營養及膠體）
    significantly during the Year. Turnover and net profit of the Group        的 收 入 於 本 年 度 大 幅 提 高。本 集 團 營 業 額
    increased by 27.6% and 201.5% respectively, reaching approximately         及 淨 利 潤 同 時 創 下 新 高，分 別 增 長 27.6% 和
    RMB27.5 billion and RMB3.9 billion, all at new highs. Gross profit         201.5%，達到約人民幣 275 億元及人民幣 39
    margin increased by 8.2 percentage points to 26.3%. These results          億元。毛利率上升 8.2 個百分點至 26.3%。這
    are a culmination of a well-ingrained performance culture focused          些結果是根深蒂固的績效文化的結晶，專注
    on continuous improvement, profitable growth, active pricing, cost         於 持 續 改 進、盈 利 增 長、主 動 定 價、成 本 管
    management and strong capital discipline.                                  理和嚴格的資本管控。

    During the Year, we accumulated a high level of US dollar position         於本年度，我們從出口銷售積累了高額的美
    generating from our export sales. In order to lower our borrowing          元頭寸。為降低借貸成本，我們全數償還了 4
    costs, we fully repaid the USD400 million syndicated bank loan. We         億美元的銀團貸款，改為借入成本較低的人
    took the RMB loans with lower borrowing costs instead. We also             民幣貸款。我們還錄得美元現金頭寸及銀團
    posted FX gains from our USD cash position and the termination of          貸款美元掉期終止所帶來的外匯收益。
    the USD swap for the syndicated bank loan.

    Reflecting strong confidence in our financial position and rewarding       出於對我們財務狀況的堅定信心及為回報各
    your support, the Board has proposed a final dividend of HK29.0            位股東的支持，董事會已建議派發每股 29.0
    cents and a special final dividend of HK4.1 cents per share. This          港 仙 的 末 期 股 息 及 每 股 4.1 港 仙 的 特 別 末 期
    results in a full-year dividend of HK69.7 cents per share (including the   股 息。因 此 全 年 股 息 為 每 股 69.7 港 仙（包 括
    interim dividend of HK32.0 cents and a special interim dividend of         中期股息每股 32.0 港仙及特別中期股息每股
    HK4.6 cents per share), with a full-year dividend payout ratio of 40%      4.6 港仙），全年派息率為 40%（二零二一年：
    (2021: 35%).                                                               35%）。

    Of course, our measure of success is defined by more than financial        當然，我們衡量成功的標準不僅僅是財務數
    numbers. We further strengthened our ESG strategy under the                據。於二零二二年，在 ESG 委員會的領導下，
    leadership of the ESG Committee. We believe that the integration of        我們進一步加強 ESG 戰略。我們深信在整個
    ESG considerations throughout our operations is essential for our          營運過程中加入 ESG 因素，對我們的可持續
    sustainable development.                                                   發展而言至關重要。

Market Overview                                                           市場概覽
Economic Environment                                                      經濟環境
According to the National Bureau of Statistics of China, China            據 中 國 國 家 統 計 局 資 料 顯 示，中 國 在 二 零
reported its GDP growth rate of 3.0% on a year-on-year basis in           二二年國內生產總值按年增長 3.0%。本年度
2022. During the Year, the global economy gradually returned to           多國陸續取消防疫措施，讓環球經濟逐步恢
normal and showed signs of recovery as a result of the lifting of         復 正 常 運 作，呈 現 復 甦 跡 象。然 而，由 於 本
pandemic measures in many countries. However, the recovery faced          年度各國新冠疫情反覆、俄烏衝突以及部分
uncertainties due to the resurgence of the COVID pandemic in various      發達市場通脹飆升，為復甦帶來了不明朗因素。
countries, the Russia-Ukraine conflict, and the soaring inflation in
some developed markets during the Year.

Major Raw Materials                                                       主要原材料
During the Year, the continuous impact of the Russia-Ukraine              年 內，俄 烏 衝 突 持 續 影 響 全 球 糧 食 市 場，多
conflict on the global food market, and export restrictions imposed       國對玉米等主要糧食實施出口限制令，令全
by many countries on staple foods (such as corn), led to a global         球 糧 食 緊 缺 導 致 食 品 價 格 上 漲。然 而，因 為
food shortage, causing an increase in food prices. However, the           中 國 的 玉 米 大 多 以 自 給 自 足 為 主，只 有 約
foreign environment had limited impact on domestic corn prices as         10% 依靠進口，國外環境對國內玉米價格影
China is mostly self-sufficient in corn, with only about 10% relying on   響 有 限。年 內 玉 米 價 格 維 持 在 高 位，原 因 在
imports. Corn prices maintained at a high level throughout the Year       於 (1) 飼 養 業 復 甦，飼 料 需 求 大 增（自 二 零 二
due to (1) the recovery of the breeding industry and the significant      零年中國非洲豬瘟疫情結束後，飼養業在二
increase in feed demand (after the end of the African swine fever         零二一年復甦，並在二零二二年保持其活躍
epidemic in China in 2020, the breeding industry recovered in 2021        走勢）；(2) 玉米加工需求維持高位；(3) 交通運
and maintained its active trend in 2022); (2) high demand from            輸受到疫情影響而受阻。
corn processing; and (3) the hindered transportation and logistics
disruption caused by the impact of the pandemic.

Coal prices in China have remained at a high level since the fourth       自二零二一年第四季度開始，中國煤炭價格
quarter in 2021. During the Year, the National Development and            持續高企。但年內國家發改委頒下若干針對
Reform Commission promulgated a number of domestic coal policies          國 內 煤 炭 政 策，適 時 調 控 煤 炭 價 格，促 使 煤
to regulate and monitor coal prices in a timely manner, so as to keep     炭價格在合理區間浮動，確保能源穩定供應，
coal prices within a reasonable range and ensure a stable supply of       使國內煤炭價格和供應得以穩定。
energy, thus stabilising domestic coal prices and supply.

     The four key steps in the risk management process are:                      風險管理程序中的四個關鍵步驟如下：

     •      Risk identification and assessment – identify the key risks          •   風險識別及評估－識別本集團的主要風
            of the Group and analyse the risk by considering the possibility         險，並透過考慮事件發生的可能性及對
            of occurrence and the impact to the Group;                               本集團的影響分析有關風險；

     •      Risk handling – adopt an appropriate risk management                 •   風險處理－就每項關鍵風險採取適當的
            strategy (i.e. risk response) for each key risk;                         風險管理策略（即風險回應）；

     •      Risk monitoring – apply monitoring mechanism to ensure               •   風 險 監 察－採 用 監 察 機 制，確 保 風 險 回
            the risk response are operated effectively;                              應行之有效；

     •      Risk reporting – summarise the result of risk assessment             •   風 險 匯 報－概 述 風 險 評 估 結 果，並 向 風
            and report to the Risk Management Team.                                  險管理小組匯報。

     In the risk management process, the top risks within the Group              在風險管理過程中會識別及評估本集團內部
     are identified and assessed; and the respective risk management             的 重 大 風 險，隨 後 管 理 層 會 討 論、協 定 及 實
     measures as well as the corresponding mitigating controls are               施相關風險管理措施和相應的紓緩控制，並
     discussed, agreed and implemented by the management. Risk                   由風險管理小組每年向董事會及審核委員會
     assessment results are reported by the Risk Management Team to              匯報有關風險評估結果。
     the Board and Audit Committee annually.

     BASIS FOR OPINION                                                          意見的基礎
     We conducted our audit in accordance with Hong Kong Standards on           我們已根據香港會計師公會頒佈的《香港審計
     Auditing (“HKSAs”) issued by the HKICPA. Our responsibilities under        準則》
                                                                                  （「香港審計準則」）進行審計。我們在該
     those standards are further described in the Auditor’s Responsibilities    等準則下承擔的責任已在本報告「核數師就審
     for the Audit of the Consolidated Financial Statements section of our      計合併財務報表承擔的責任」部分中作進一步
     report.                                                                    闡述。

Key Audit Matter                                                             關鍵審計事項
Revenue recognition                                                          收入確認

Refer to note 2.22 and note 5 to the Group’s consolidated financial          請參閱 貴集團合併財務報表附註 2.22 及附
statements.                                                                  註 5。

Revenue from sales of goods amounted to RMB27,475 million for the            截至二零二二年十二月三十一日止年度，商
year ended 31 December 2022. Revenue is recognised when control              品銷售收入為人民幣 274.75 億元。相關商品
of the underlying products has been transferred to the customers.            控制權轉移至客戶時，確認相關收入。

We focused on this area due to the huge volume of revenue                    我們考慮到於眾多不同地點與眾多客戶進行
transactions generated from the sales of numerous kinds of products          交 易，銷 售 多 種 商 品 且 交 易 量 巨 大，我 們 對
to a large number of customers that occurred in many different               該 領 域 重 點 關 注。對 於 出 口 銷 售，商 品 控 制
locations. In relation to export sales, it usually takes more time for the   權轉移給購貨方通常比國內銷售耗時更久，
control of products to pass over to customers than domestic sales.           故收入是否已確認在正確報告期間存在潛在
There is potential risk of misstatement in relation to whether revenue       錯報風險。
is recognised in the correct reporting periods.

How our audit addressed the Key Audit Matter                                 我們的審計如何處理關鍵審計事項
We evaluated and validated management’s key controls that                    我們對 貴集團銷售流程中管理層現有的關
are present in the Group’s sales process from end-to-end, from               鍵控制進行評估及驗證，包括從客戶訂單批
customer order’s approval, sales recording, all the way through to           准、記錄銷售一直到現金收據及客戶記錄對
reconciliations with cash receipts and customers’ records.                   賬整個過程。

We conducted testing of sales revenue recorded covering different            我們對不同產品、地點及客戶的銷售收入執
products, locations and customers, by examining the relevant                 行 了 測 試，檢 查 銷 售 相 關 支 持 性 文 件，包 括
supporting documents including customer orders, goods delivery               客 戶 訂 單、發 貨 單 及 報 關 單。此 外，我 們 對
notes and customs declaration notices. In addition, we confirmed             資產負債表日的特定客戶應收賬款餘額及全
certain customers’ receivable balances at the balance sheet date             年交易額進行了發函確認，如果回函金額同
and their transaction amounts during the year, and tested the                賬面金額存在差異，則對差異調節表進行測
reconciliations between the book amounts and confirmed amounts               試。以上抽樣測試項目系通過考慮交易金額、
if these were different. The items tested were selected on a sample          交易性質及客戶特徵等基礎上挑選的。
basis by considering the amount, nature and characteristics of those
customers.

Our work also included testing of a sample of manual revenue-                我們的工作還包括通過詢問管理層該些分錄
related journal entries by inquiring management of the nature of these       的性質以及檢查其支持性文件來測試被抽樣
journals and inspection of the supporting documents.                         的與收入相關的手工分錄。

Furthermore, one of our focused audit efforts was testing export             並且，我們的審計重點之一是對資產負債表
sales transactions that took place shortly before and after the balance      日前後不久發生的出口銷售交易進行截止性
sheet date, by reconciling recognised revenue with the goods delivery        測 試，通 過 檢 查 發 貨 單 和 報 關 單，與 已 確 認
notes and customs declaration notices to assess whether revenue              收入進行對比，評估收入是否確認在正確報
was recognised in the correct reporting periods.                             告期間。

We found the Group’s sales transactions being tested were                    我們認為已測試的 貴集團銷售交易的確認
recognised in a manner consistent with the Group’s revenue                   同貴集團收入確認的會計政策保持一致。
recognition accounting policy.

  Fufeng Group Limited and its subsidiaries manufacture and sell           阜豐集團有限公司及其附屬公司從事製

  starch-based products. The Group has manufacturing plants                澱粉產品。本集團於中國山東省、陝西省、
  in Shandong Province, Shaanxi Province, Jiangsu Province,                江 蘇 省、黑 龍 江 省、內 蒙 古 自 治 區 及 新
  Heilongjiang Province, Inner Mongolia Autonomous Region and              疆維吾爾自治區設有生產廠房，主要向
  Xinjiang Uygur Autonomous Region of the PRC and sells mainly             中國客戶進行銷售。
  to customers located in the PRC.

  The Company is a limited liability company incorporated in               本公司為於開曼群島註冊成立的有限公
  the Cayman Islands. The address of its registered office is              司，其註冊辦事處地址為 Cricket Square,

  Hong Kong Limited.                                                       上市。

  2.6 Property, plant and equipment (Continued)                         2.6 物業、廠房及設備（續）
      Depreciation on property, plant and equipment, except for             除 在 建 工 程 外，物 業、廠 房 及 設 備
      construction in progress, is calculated using the straight-           的折舊於其估計可使用年期，以直
      line method to allocate their costs to their residual values          線法分配其成本至其剩餘價值，計
      over their estimated useful lives, as follows:                        算年期如下：

      Plant and building                               15–20 years          廠房及大樓                 15–20 年
      Machinery                                         8–10 years          機器                     8–10 年
      Furniture and fixtures                             3–8 years          傢俬及裝置                   3–8 年
      Vehicles                                           5–8 years          汽車                      5–8 年

      The assets’ residual values and useful lives are reviewed,            於各報告期末會檢討資產剩餘價值
      and adjusted if appropriate, at the end of each reporting             及 可 使 用 年 期，並 作 出 調 整（如 適
      period.                                                               用）。

      An asset’s carrying amount is written down immediately                倘一項資產的賬面值大於其估計可
      to its recoverable amount if the asset’s carrying amount is           收 回 金 額（附 註 2.8 ），則 該 項 資 產
      greater than its estimated recoverable amount (Note 2.8).             賬面值將立即撇減至其可收回金額。

      Gains and losses on disposals are determined by                       出售收益及虧損乃比較所得款項與
      comparing the proceeds with carrying amount. These are                賬面值後釐定，並計入合併利潤表
      included in the consolidated income statement under “Other           「其他利得╱（損失）一淨額」內。
      gains/(losses) – net”.

            the straight-line method to allocate the cost of patents              的估計收益期間以直線法將專

            beneficial period of 10 to 20 years.

            and therefore are all classified as current.                          付，故均分類為流動部分。


            Trade receivables are recognised initially at the amount              應收貿易賬款按可無條件獲得的對
            of consideration that is unconditional unless they contain            價金額進行初始確認，但當其包含

            the objective to collect the contractual cash flows and               的目的是收取合同現金流量，因此
            therefore measures them subsequently at amortised                     後續使用實際利率法按攤銷成本計
            cost using the effective interest method. See Note 21 for             量應收貿易賬款。有關本集團就應

            impairment policies.                                                  策，請參閱附註 3.1。

  2.17 Borrowings                                                        2.17 借貸
      Borrowings are initially recognised at fair value, net of             借貸初步以公平值扣除所產生交易
      transaction costs incurred. Borrowings are subsequently               成本確認。借貸其後按攤餘成本列
      carried at amortised cost; any difference between the                 賬；所得款項（扣除交易成本）與贖
      proceeds (net of transaction costs) and the redemption                回價值間的任何差額，乃以實際利
      value is recognised in the consolidated income statement              息法於借貸期間在合併利潤表確認。
      over the period of the borrowings using the effective                 倘很有可能提取部分或全部融資，
      interest method. Fees paid on the establishment of loan               則設立貸款融資時支付的費用確認
      facilities are recognised as transaction costs of the loan to         為貸款的交易成本。在此情況下，
      the extent that it is probable that some or all of the facility       該費用可遞延入賬直至提取貸款為
      will be drawn down. In this case, the fee is deferred until           止。如並無證據證明部分或全部融
      the draw-down occurs. To the extent there is no evidence              資很有可能被提取，則該項費用資
      that it is probable that some or all of the facility will be          本化以作為流動資金服務的預付款
      drawn down, the fee is capitalised as a pre-payment for               項，並於有關融資期間攤銷。
      liquidity services and amortised over the period of the
      facility to which it relates.

      Borrowings are classified as current liabilities unless the           除非本集團有權無條件將債務遞延
      Group has an unconditional right to defer settlement of the           至報告期間結算日後至少十二個月
      liability for at least 12 months after the end of the reporting       方始償還，否則借貸將分類為流動
      period.                                                               負債。

      General and specific borrowing costs directly attributable            購 建 或 生 產 合 資 格 資 產（即 需 經 較
      to the acquisition, construction or production of qualifying          長時間方能達致擬定用途或出售狀
      assets, which are assets that necessarily take a substantial          態 的 資 產）直 接 應 佔 的 一 般 及 特 定
      period of time to get ready for their intended use or sale,           借貸成本，均計入該等資產的成本，
      are added to the cost of those assets, until such time as             直至該等資產基本上達致其擬定用
      the assets are substantially ready for their intended use or          途或出售狀態為止。

      assets is deducted from the borrowing costs eligible for              自合資格資本化的借貸成本中扣除。
      capitalisation.

      Other borrowing costs are expensed in the period in which             其他借貸成本於其產生期間費用化。

        2.19 Current and deferred income tax                                  2.19 即期及遞延所得稅
            The income tax expense or credit for the period is the tax          期內所得稅開支或抵免指根據各司
            payable on the current period’s taxable income based on             法權區的適用所得稅率按即期應課
            the applicable income tax rate for each jurisdiction adjusted       稅收入支付的稅項，並根據由於暫
            by changes in deferred income tax assets and liabilities            時性差異及未使用稅務虧損而導致
            attributable to temporary differences and to unused tax             的遞延所得稅資產和負債變動作出
            losses.                                                             調整。

            (a)   Current income tax                                            (a)   即期所得稅
                  The current income tax charge is calculated on                      即期所得稅支出根據本公司及
                  the basis of the tax laws enacted or substantively                  其附屬公司營運及產生應課稅
                  enacted at the balance sheet date in the countries                  收入所在國家於資產負債表日
                  where the Company and its subsidiaries operate and                  已頒佈或實質頒佈的稅務法例
                  generate taxable income. Management periodically                    計算。管理層就適用稅務法例
                  evaluates positions taken in tax returns with respect to            以詮釋為準的情況定期評估稅
                  situations in which applicable tax regulation is subject            項收益狀況，並在適用情況下
                  to interpretation. It establishes provisions where                  根據預期須向稅務機關支付的
                  appropriate on the basis of amounts expected to be                  稅款設定撥備。
                  paid to the tax authorities.

                  Deferred income tax is recognised, using the liability              遞延所得稅採用負債法就資產
                  method, on temporary differences arising between the                及負債的稅基與資產及負債在
                  tax bases of assets and liabilities and their carrying              合併財務報表內之賬面值產生
                  amounts in the consolidated financial statements.                   的暫時性差額進行確認。然而，
                  However, the deferred income tax is not accounted                   倘遞延所得稅乃因資產或負債
                  for if it arises from initial recognition of an asset               在一宗交易（業務合併除外）中
                  or liability in a transaction other than a business                 獲初步確認而產生，並且於交
                  combination that at the time of the transaction affects             易時對會計或應課稅溢利或虧
                  neither accounting nor taxable profit or loss. Deferred             損均無影響，則遞延所得稅不
                  income tax is determined using tax rates (and laws)                 計算入賬。遞延所得稅採用在
                  that have been enacted or substantially enacted by                  資產負債表日已頒佈或實質頒
                  the balance sheet date and are expected to apply                    佈之稅率（及法例）釐定，並預
                  when the related deferred income tax asset is realised              期在變現有關遞延所得稅資產
                  or the deferred income tax liability is settled.                    或償付遞延所得稅負債時將會
                                                                                      適用。

                  Deferred income tax assets are recognised only if it is             遞延所得稅資產僅在很有可能
                  probable that future taxable amounts will be available              將未來應課稅溢利與可動用之
                  to utilise those temporary differences and losses.                  暫時差額及虧損抵銷情況下確
                                                                                      認。

        2.22 Revenue recognition                                             2.22 收益確認

            The Group manufactures and sells a range of fermentation-          本集團在全球市場製造及銷售一系

            to sell the products, and there is no unfulfilled obligation       未 履 行 義 務 時，確 認 銷 售。當 產 品
            that could affect the customer’s acceptance of the                 交付到指定地點或由客戶指定的承

            by carriers designated by customers for domestic sales,            滅失的風險已轉移給客戶，及客戶
            or have been shipped on board for overseas sales, the              已根據銷售合約接納產品，接納條
            risks of obsolescence and loss have been transferred               文已失效，或本集團有客觀證據證

            the products in accordance with the sales contract, the

  2.25 Leases (Continued)                                               2.25 租賃（續）
      The lease payments are discounted using the interest                  租賃付款使用租賃中內含的利率進
      rate implicit in the lease. If that rate cannot be readily            行貼現。倘無法輕易確定該利率（為
      determined, which is generally the case for leases in the             本 集 團 租 賃 的 一 般 情 況），則 使 用
      group, the lessee’s incremental borrowing rate is used,               承租人的增量借款利率，即個別承
      being the rate that the lessee would have to pay to borrow            租人在類似經濟環境中按類似條款
      the funds necessary to obtain an asset of similar value in            及條件借入獲得與使用權資產具有
      a similar economic environment with similar terms and                 類似價值的資產所需資金而必須支
      conditions.                                                           付的利率。

      To determine the incremental borrowing rate, the Group:               為釐定增量借款利率，本集團應：

      •    where possible, uses recent third-party financing                •   在可能情況下，以承租人最近
           received by the individual lessee as a starting point,               收到的第三方融資為起點，並
           adjusted to reflect changes in financing conditions                  進行調整以反映融資條件自收
           since third party financing was received.                            到第三方融資後的變化。

      Lease payment is allocated between the principal and                  租賃付款額於本金及融資費用之間
      finance cost. The finance cost is charged to profit or loss           作出分配。融資費用在租賃期內計
      over the lease period so as to produce a constant periodic            入損益，以按照固定的周期性利率
      rate of interest on the remaining balance of the liability for        對各期間負債餘額計算利息。
      each period.

      Right-of-use assets are measured at cost comprising the               使用權資產按成本計量，包括以下
      following:                                                            各項：

      •    the amount of the initial measurement of lease liability,        •   租賃負債的初始計量金額；及
           and

      •    any lease payments made at or before the                         •   在開始日期或之前支付的任何
           commencement date.                                                   租賃付款。

      Right-of-use assets are generally depreciated over the                使用權資產一般於資產的使用壽命
      shorter of the asset’s useful life and the lease term on a            與租賃期兩者孰短的期間按直線法
      straight-line basis.                                                  計提折舊。

      Payments associated with short-term leases of warehouse               與短期倉庫租賃相關的付款額按直
      are recognised on a straight-line basis as an expense in              線法於損益中確認為開支。短期租
      profit or loss. Short-term leases are leases with a lease             賃 是 指 租 賃 期 為 12 個 月 或 小 於 12

           (c)   Liquidity risk                                                              (c)     流動資金風險
                 Prudent liquidity risk management includes maintaining                              審慎的流動資金風險管理包括
                 sufficient cash and available credit facilities to meet                             保持充裕現金及可動用的信貸
                 obligations when they arise.                                                        融資，以應付有關風險。

                 Management monitors the funding requirements of                                     管理層監控本集團的資金需求
                 the Group and the availability of credit facilities in order                        及信貸融資的可用性，以確保
                 to ensure the liquidity of the Group.                                               本集團資金流動性。

                 The table below analyses the Group’s financial                                      下表根據合約到期日按相關到

       3.2 Capital management                                                  3.2 資本管理
           (a)   Risk management                                                 (a)   風險管理
                 The Group’s objectives when managing capital are to                   本集團的資本管理目標為

                 •      safeguard their ability to continue as a going                 •   保障其按持續經營基準繼
                        concern, so that they can continue to provide                      續營運的能力，因此彼等
                        returns for shareholders and benefits for other                    可繼續為股東帶來回報，
                        stakeholders, and                                                  同時兼顧其他權益持有人
                                                                                           的利益，及

                 •      maintain an optimal capital structure to reduce                •   維持最佳資本結構以減低
                        the cost of capital.                                               資金成本。

                 In order to maintain or adjust the capital structure,                 為維持或調整資本架構，本集
                 the Group may adjust the amount of dividends paid                     團或會調整支付予股東的股息
                 to shareholders, return capital to shareholders, issue                金 額、向 股 東 發 還 資 本、發 行
                 new shares or sell assets to reduce debt.                             新股或出售資產以減低債務。

                 The Group monitors capital on the basis of the gearing                本集團根據資產負債比率監察
                 ratio. This ratio is equal to total debt divided by total             其資本。該比率相等於債務總
                 assets at the end of the corresponding year.                          額除以上各對應年度年結日資
                                                                                       產總值。

                 Total debt includes current and non-current bank                      於二零二二年十二月三十一日，
                 borrowings and non-current borrowings from third                      債務總額包括即期及非即期銀
                 parties as at 31 December 2022.                                       行借貸以及來自第三方的非即
                                                                                       期借貸。

                 The Group’s strategy is to maintain the gearing ratio                 本集團的策略為將資產負債比
                 below 40% (2021: 40%).The gearing ratios at 31                        率 維 持 於 40% 以 下（二 零 二 一
                 December 2022 and 2021 were as follows:                               年：40% ）。於 二 零 二 二 年 及

                   The Group’s policy is to recognise transfers into                        本集團政策旨在確認於報
                   and transfers out of fair value hierarchy levels as                      告期末公平值等級水平的
                   at the end of the reporting period.                                      轉入及轉出情況。

                   Level 1: The fair value of financial instruments                         第一級：在活躍市場上買
                   traded in active markets (such as publicly traded                        賣 的 金 融 工 具（如 公 開 買
                   derivatives, and equity securities) is based on                          賣 衍 生 工 具 及 股 本 證 券）
                   quoted market prices at the end of the reporting                         的公平值乃按於報告期末
                   period. The quoted market price used for                                 的市場報價釐定。本集團
                   financial assets held by the Group is the current                        所持有金融資產採用的市
                   bid price. These instruments are included in level                       場報價為當時買盤價。該
                   1.                                                                       等工具計入第一級。

                   Level 2: The fair value of financial instruments                         第二級：未在活躍市場上
                   that are not traded in an active market (for                             買 賣 的 金 融 工 具（例 如 場
                   example, over-the-counter derivatives) is                                外 衍 生 工 具）的 公 平 值 採
                   determined using valuation techniques which                              用估值技術釐定。該等估
                   maximise the use of observable market data                               值技術盡量採用可觀察市
                   and rely as little as possible on entity-specific                        場數據，並盡可能不倚賴
                   estimates. If all significant inputs required to                         實體特定估計。倘計算工
                   fair value an instrument are observable, the                             具公平值所需的重大輸入
                   instrument is included in level 2.                                       數據均可觀察，則該工具
                                                                                            計入第二級。

                   Level 3: If one or more of the significant inputs                        第三級：倘一項或多項重
                   is not based on observable market data, the                              大輸入數據並非以可觀察
                   instrument is included in level 3. This is the case                      市場數據為基礎，則該工
                   for unlisted equity securities.                                          具計入第三級。對於非上
                                                                                            市股本證券而言便是如此。

  4. CRITICAL ACCOUNTING ESTIMATES AND                                     4. 重要會計估計及判斷
     JUDGMENTS
       The preparation of financial statements requires the use of          財務報表之編製需要使用會計估計，根
       accounting estimates which, by definition, will seldom equal the     據定義，該等會計估計很少會與實際結
       actual results. Management also needs to exercise judgment in        果相同。管理層亦需在應用本集團的會
       applying the Group’s accounting policies.                            計政策時作出判斷。

       Estimates and judgments are continually evaluated and are            對估計及判斷作出持續評估，並以過往
       based on historical experience and other factors, including          經驗及其他因素作基礎，包括在目前情
       expectations of future events that are believed to be reasonable     況下對未來事件的合理預測。
       under the circumstances.

       The Group makes estimates and assumptions concerning the             本集團對未來作出估計及假設。所得會
       future. The resulting accounting estimates will, by definition,      計估計一如其定義甚少與相關的實際結
       seldom equal the related actual results. The estimates and           果相同。以下所述的估計及假設有相當
       assumptions that have a significant risk of causing a material       大風險會引致須於下一個財政年度對資
       adjustment to the carrying amounts of assets and liabilities         產及負債的賬面值作出重大調整。
       within the next financial year are described below.

            conditions as well as forward looking estimates at the end        計算的輸入數據。所用的主要假設
            of each reporting period. Details of the key assumptions          及輸入數據的詳情於附註 3.1(b) 披露。
            and inputs used are disclosed in Note 3.1(b).

  25. OTHER RESERVES (Continued)                                           25. 其他儲備（續）
       (a) Capital reserve                                                   (a) 資本儲備
           It mainly represents the reserve arising from the Group’s           資本儲備主要指本集團於二零零六
           reorganisation completed in July 2006.                              年七月完成重組後產生的儲備。

       (b) Statutory reserve                                                 (b) 法定儲備
           In accordance with the PRC regulations and the articles             根據中國規例及組成本集團的旗下
           of the association of the PRC companies comprising the              各中國公司的組織章程細則規定，
           Group, before distributing the net profit of each year, each        在分派各年純利前，每間在中國註
           of the companies registered in the PRC is required to               冊的公司均須按中國會計準則規定，
           set aside 10% of its statutory net profit for the year after        於對銷任何上一年度虧損後，將該
           offsetting any prior year’s losses as determined under the          年度的法定純利 10% 撥入法定盈餘
           PRC accounting regulations to the statutory surplus reserve         儲備基金。倘該儲備的結餘達到各
           fund. When the balance of such reserve reaches 50% of               公司股本的 50%，其後可自行決定
           each company’s share capital, any further appropriation is          任何撥調。法定盈餘儲備基金可用
           optional. The statutory surplus reserve fund can be utilised        以對銷過往年度虧損或發行紅股，
           to offset prior years’ losses or to issue bonus shares,             惟有關儲備結餘不少於發行紅股後
           provided that the balance of such reserve is not less than          實體註冊資本的 25%。
           25% of the entity’s registered capital after the bonus issue.

29. DEFERRED INCOME TAX                                                  29. 遞延所得稅
  Deferred income tax assets and liabilities are offset when there         當具有將即期所得稅資產與即期所得稅
  is a legally enforceable right to offset current income tax assets       負債抵銷的合法強制執行權，以及當遞
  against current income tax liabilities and when the deferred             延所得稅資產及負債與同一稅務機關就
  income tax assets and liabilities relate to income taxed levied          該應課稅實體或不同應課稅實體徵收的
  by the same taxation authority on either the taxable entity or           所得稅有關，而有關方面擬按淨額基準
  different taxable entities where there is an intention to settle the     償付結餘時，遞延所得稅資產與負債將
  balances on a net basis. The deferred income tax assets and              予抵銷。遞延所得稅資產及負債如下：
